The printed outfit runs from the neckline to the hem and from one sleeve hem to the other, its shoulders on the shirt's shoulder seams, showing a black cassock with a white surplice. The outfit is a sexy version in glossy tight material, off-the-shoulder. The print wraps all the way round, the back of the shirt shows the rear of the outfit from the back collar to the hem and along both sleeves. The outfit's own cloth covers the whole shirt as one continuous print, so shoulders, sleeves and body are all in the outfit's colours. It is an ordinary T-shirt in shape, with a straight open hem and sleeves cut to match the outfit's sleeves. The printed top garment continues down the shirt to its straight hem, with at most a belt or sash at the waist. The print is cut exactly to the shirt, neck, shoulders, armholes, sleeves and hem are all covered in the outfit's own fabric. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided shirt shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. A short-sleeved shirt is used, and the outfit is printed as short sleeves that end at the shirt's sleeve hems. All textures, transparencies and sheens are printed patterns on solid cotton. Only the outfit's fabric, trims and fastenings are pictured, spread across the entire shirt as a continuous pattern. It is a trompe-l'oeil T-shirt, a plain cotton shirt pretending to be the outfit. To read as a costume rather than a plain top, it adds its pockets, flaps and trims, the footwear's trim colours as stripes on the cuffs and hem and the lower garment's colours and pattern as a band across the hem, bold and large enough to identify the outfit from across a room.

Every panel of the shirt, front, shoulders and sleeves, is a panel of the outfit, showing a woven flax cloak. The outfit is a sexy version with a corset bodice, deep cleavage. The print wraps all the way round, the back of the shirt shows the rear of the outfit from the back collar to the hem and along both sleeves. The outfit's fabric runs seamlessly over the shoulders and down the sleeves, every part of the shirt in the outfit's own colours and textures. The shirt keeps the outline of an ordinary T-shirt, round neckline, straight side seams, sleeves, and a straight open hem. The shirt is an ordinary T-shirt ending in a straight bottom hem, and the garment printed on it runs straight down the whole shirt to that hem. The outfit's collar meets the real collar band, its shoulders sit on the real shoulder seams and its sleeves fill the real sleeves, all in one piece of printed cloth. The outfit is stretched to the shirt's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. The shirt is a short-sleeved T-shirt, and the outfit's sleeves are printed as short sleeves ending exactly at the shirt's sleeve hems. The shirt's sleeves are tailored to the outfit's cut, wide or flared where the outfit's sleeves flare, ending at the same slant or curve, with the print filling the whole sleeve shape. The shirt is solid opaque cotton and every texture and sheen is a printed picture. The picture is only the garment, its cloth, seams, buttons and trims laid flat across the whole shirt like a sewing pattern. It also shows a printed collar continuing the outfit up to the neckline and printed cuffs at the sleeve hems. It is a trompe-l'oeil T-shirt, a plain cotton shirt pretending to be the outfit. It carries enough of the costume to be unmistakable, the collar, neckwear and fastenings of the outfit, the footwear's trim colours as stripes on the cuffs and hem and the belt, straps and buckles of the outfit, all in the outfit's own colours.
